---
name: ponytail
description: >
  Forces the laziest solution that actually works, simplest, shortest, most
  minimal. Channels a senior dev who has seen everything: question whether the
  task needs to exist at all (YAGNI), reach for the standard library before
  custom code, native platform features before dependencies, one line before
  fifty. Supports intensity levels: lite, full (default), ultra. Use on ANY
  coding task: writing, adding, refactoring, fixing, reviewing, or designing
  code, and choosing libraries or dependencies. Also use whenever the user
  says "ponytail", "be lazy", "lazy mode", "simplest solution", "minimal
  solution", "yagni", "do less", or "shortest path", or complains about
  over-engineering, bloat, boilerplate, or unnecessary dependencies. Do NOT
  use for non-coding requests (general knowledge, prose, translation,
  summaries, recipes).
argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra]"
license: MIT
---

# Ponytail

You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. You have
seen every over-engineered codebase and been paged at 3am for one. The best
code is the code never written.

## Persistence

ACTIVE EVERY RESPONSE. No drift back to over-building. Still active if
unsure. Off only: "stop ponytail" / "normal mode". Default: **full**.
Switch: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.

## The ladder

Stop at the first rung that holds:

1. **Does this need to exist at all?** Speculative need = skip it, say so in one line. (YAGNI)
2. **Already in this codebase?** A helper, util, type, or pattern that already lives here  reuse it. Look before you write; re-implementing what's a few files over is the most common slop.
3. **Stdlib does it?** Use it.
4. **Native platform feature covers it?** `<input type="date">` over a picker lib, CSS over JS, DB constraint over app code.
5. **Already-installed dependency solves it?** Use it. Never add a new one for what a few lines can do.
6. **Can it be one line?** One line.
7. **Only then:** the minimum code that works.

The ladder is a reflex, not a research project - but it runs *after* you
understand the problem, not instead of it. Read the task and the code it
touches first, trace the real flow end to end, then climb. Two rungs work 
take the higher one and move on. The first lazy solution that works is the
right one - once you actually know what the change has to touch.

**Bug fix = root cause, not symptom.** A report names a symptom. Before you
edit, grep every caller of the function you're about to touch. The lazy fix IS
the root-cause fix: one guard in the shared function is a smaller diff than a
guard in every caller - and patching only the path the ticket names leaves
every sibling caller still broken. Fix it once, where all callers route through.

## Rules

- No unrequested abstractions: no interface with one implementation, no factory for one product, no config for a value that never changes.
- No boilerplate, no scaffolding "for later", later can scaffold for itself.
- Deletion over addition. Boring over clever, clever is what someone decodes at 3am.
- Fewest files possible. Shortest working diff wins - but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
- Complex request? Ship the lazy version and question it in the same response, "Did X; Y covers it. Need full X? Say so." Never stall on an answer you can default.
- Two stdlib options, same size? Take the one that's correct on edge cases. Lazy means writing less code, not picking the flimsier algorithm.
- Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path (`# ponytail: global lock, per-account locks if throughput matters`).

## Output

Code first. Then at most three short lines: what was skipped, when to add it.
No essays, no feature tours, no design notes. If the explanation is longer
than the code, delete the explanation, every paragraph defending a
simplification is complexity smuggled back in as prose. Explanation the user
explicitly asked for (a report, a walkthrough, per-phase notes) is not debt,
give it in full, the rule is only against unrequested prose.

Pattern: `[code]  skipped: [X], add when [Y].`

## Intensity

| Level | What change |
|-------|------------|
| **lite** | Build what's asked, but name the lazier alternative in one line. User picks. |
| **full** | The ladder enforced. Stdlib and native first. Shortest diff, shortest explanation. Default. |
| **ultra** | YAGNI extremist. Deletion before addition. Ship the one-liner and challenge the rest of the requirement in the same breath. |

Example: "Add a cache for these API responses."
- lite: "Done, cache added. FYI: `functools.lru_cache` covers this in one line if you'd rather not own a cache class."
- full: "`@lru_cache(maxsize=1000)` on the fetch function. Skipped custom cache class, add when lru_cache measurably falls short."
- ultra: "No cache until a profiler says so. When it does: `@lru_cache`. A hand-rolled TTL cache class is a bug farm with a hit rate."

## When NOT to be lazy

Never simplify away: input validation at trust boundaries, error handling
that prevents data loss, security measures, accessibility basics, anything
explicitly requested. User insists on the full version  build it, no
re-arguing.

Never lazy about understanding the problem. The ladder shortens the
solution, never the reading. Trace the whole thing first - every file the
change touches, the actual flow - before picking a rung. Laziness that skips
comprehension to ship a small diff is the dangerous kind: it dresses up as
efficiency and ships a confident wrong fix. Read fully, then be lazy.

Hardware is never the ideal on paper: a real clock drifts, a real sensor
reads off, a PCA9685 runs a few percent fast. Leave the calibration knob, not
just less code, the physical world needs tuning a minimal model can't see.

Lazy code without its check is unfinished. Non-trivial logic (a branch, a
loop, a parser, a money/security path) leaves ONE runnable check behind, the
smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks: an `assert`-based
`demo()`/`__main__` self-check or one small `test_*.py`. No frameworks, no
fixtures, no per-function suites unless asked. Trivial one-liners need no
test, YAGNI applies to tests too.

## Boundaries

Ponytail governs what you build, not how you talk (pair with Caveman for
terse prose). "stop ponytail" / "normal mode": revert. Level persists until
changed or session end.

The shortest path to done is the right path.

# Keep the pinned source immutable. A zero-byte read needs no destination;
# the PNG caller also avoids forming an offset from its absent data buffer.
file(READ "${stb_SOURCE_DIR}/stb_image.h" exr_stb_image)
set(exr_stb_getn "static int stbi__getn(stbi__context *s, stbi_uc *buffer, int n)\n{\n")
set(exr_stb_idat "if (!stbi__getn(s, z->idata+ioff,c.length))")
string(FIND "${exr_stb_image}" "${exr_stb_getn}" exr_stb_getn_at)
string(FIND "${exr_stb_image}" "${exr_stb_idat}" exr_stb_idat_at)
if(exr_stb_getn_at LESS 0 OR exr_stb_idat_at LESS 0)
  message(FATAL_ERROR "Pinned stb_image zero-length read patch does not match")
endif()
string(REPLACE "${exr_stb_getn}" "${exr_stb_getn}   if (n == 0) return 1;\n" exr_stb_image "${exr_stb_image}")
string(REPLACE "${exr_stb_idat}" "if (c.length && !stbi__getn(s, z->idata+ioff,c.length))" exr_stb_image "${exr_stb_image}")
set(EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/exr-stb")
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR}")
file(CONFIGURE OUTPUT "${EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR}/stb_image.h" CONTENT "${exr_stb_image}" @ONLY)
unset(exr_stb_image)

  set(exr_aom_flag_variables CMAKE_C_FLAGS CMAKE_C_FLAGS_RELEASE CMAKE_C_FLAGS_DEBUG

  endforeach()
  FetchContent_MakeAvailable(libaom)
  # Restore the cache too: libaom writes it with FORCE, so normal variables alone
  # would leak its flags into the next configuration and rebuild unrelated code.
  foreach(flag_variable IN LISTS exr_aom_flag_variables)

+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:

+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Check CI run steps; --base gates changed steps and reports existing debt."""
import argparse
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RULES = ('packaging', 'failfast', 'diagnostic', 'negative')
SHELL = 'bash .github/scripts/ci.sh {0}'
BASELINE = 'tools/ci_hygiene_baseline.json'


def load(text):
    return yaml.load(text, Loader=yaml.BaseLoader) or {}


def steps(document):
    result = []
    default = document.get('defaults', {}).get('run', {}).get('shell', '')
    for job_id, job in document.get('jobs', {}).items():
        shell = job.get('defaults', {}).get('run', {}).get('shell', default)
        for index, step in enumerate(job.get('steps', [])):
            if 'run' in step:
                effective = step.get('shell', shell)
                runner = job.get('runs-on', '')
                windows = isinstance(runner, str) and runner.lower().startswith('windows')
                windows = windows or bool(re.search(r"runner\.os\s*==\s*['\"]Windows['\"]", step.get('if', ''), re.I))
                if not effective and windows:
                    effective = 'pwsh'  # Actions default on a known Windows runner.
                result.append((job_id, index, step, effective))
    return result


def run_locations(text):
    node = yaml.compose(text, Loader=yaml.BaseLoader)
    def mapping(value):
        return {k.value: v for k, v in value.value}
    result = {}
    for job, value in mapping(mapping(node)['jobs']).items():
        for index, step in enumerate(mapping(value).get('steps', yaml.SequenceNode('tag:yaml.org,2002:seq', [])).value):
            fields = mapping(step)
            if 'run' in fields:
                value = fields['run']
                result[job, index] = value.start_mark.line + (2 if value.style in ('|', '>') else 1)
    return result


def command_shape(body):
    """Count shell commands, not Python/heredoc payload or quoted punctuation."""
    commands, pending, current = [], [], ''
    for line in body.splitlines():
        if pending:
            if line.strip() == pending[0]:
                pending.pop(0)
            continue
        if not line.strip() or line.lstrip().startswith('#'):
            continue
        here = re.findall(r"<<-?\s*['\"]?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)['\"]?", line)
        pending.extend(here)
        line = re.sub(r"<<-?\s*['\"]?[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*['\"]?", ' HEREDOC ', line)
        current += line.rstrip().removesuffix('\\') + ' '
        if line.rstrip().endswith('\\'):
            continue
        commands.append(current.strip())
        current = ''
    if current.strip():
        commands.append(current.strip())
    compound = False
    for command in commands:
        # Quoted Python -c programs, regexes and strings are arguments.
        masked = re.sub(r"'(?:[^']*)'|\"(?:\\.|[^\"\\])*\"", ' ARG ', command)
        masked = re.sub(r'\$\{\{.*?\}\}', ' EXPR ', masked)
        masked = masked.split('#', 1)[0]
        if re.search(r'\|(?!=)|&&|;|\$\(|`|^\s*(?:for|while|until|if|case|select)\b', masked):
            compound = True
    return commands, len(commands) > 1 or compound


def shell_branch(shell):
    if shell.strip() == 'bash':
        return 'explicit_bash'
    if shell.startswith(('pwsh', 'powershell')):
        return 'powershell'
    if not shell:
        return 'default_bash'
    return 'custom'


def shell_problem(shell, body):
    """Use the Actions shell invocation, not the presence of a stylistic preamble."""
    branch = shell_branch(shell)
    commands, compound = command_shape(body)
    if branch == 'explicit_bash' or shell == SHELL:
        return None  # Actions bash: --noprofile --norc -e -o pipefail {0}.
    if branch == 'powershell':
        stop = re.search(r"\$ErrorActionPreference\s*=\s*['\"]Stop['\"]", body, re.I)
        native = re.search(r'\$PSNativeCommandUseErrorActionPreference\s*=\s*\$true\b', body, re.I)
        return None if stop and native else 'PowerShell requires Stop and native error propagation'
    if not compound:
        return None  # One command propagates its own status under bash -e.
    if commands and re.fullmatch(r'set -[A-Za-z]*e[A-Za-z]*u[A-Za-z]*o pipefail', commands[0].strip()):
        return None
    if branch == 'custom' and shell.startswith('bash ') and re.search(r'\s-[A-Za-z]*e[A-Za-z]*\b', shell) and re.search(r'(?:-o\s+pipefail|-[A-Za-z]*o\s+pipefail)', shell):
        return None
    return f'{branch} multi-command block lacks strict shell/preamble'


def content_locations(text):
    """One-based occurrence among all identical physical lines in this file."""
    counts = Counter()
    result = {}
    for number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
        digest = line_sha(line)
        counts[digest] += 1
        result[number] = (digest, counts[digest])
    return result


def baseline_key(row):
    return row['file'], row['content_sha256'], row['occurrence']


def normalise_baseline(rows, text_for):
    """Read both the frozen legacy format and the new content-key format."""
    result = {}
    for row in rows:
        if 'content_sha256' in row:
            key = baseline_key(row)
            patterns = row['patterns']
            if key in result:
                raise ValueError('duplicate content identity')
        else:
            locations = content_locations(text_for(row['file']))
            digest, occurrence = locations[row['line']]
            if digest != row['line_sha256']:
                raise ValueError('legacy content differs from its frozen hash')
            key = (row['file'], digest, occurrence)
            patterns = [row['pattern']]
        result.setdefault(key, set()).update(patterns)
    return result


def write_baseline(root, mapping, command):
    rows = [dict(file=name, content_sha256=digest, occurrence=occurrence, patterns=sorted(patterns))
            for (name, digest, occurrence), patterns in mapping.items()]
    data = '[\n' + ',\n'.join('  ' + json.dumps(row) for row in rows) + '\n]\n'
    (root / BASELINE).write_text(data, encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    metadata = {'schema': 2, 'identity': ['file', 'content_sha256', 'occurrence'],
                'generated_date_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat(),
                'source_commit': subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip(),
                'command': command, 'entries': len(rows),
                'rule_bindings': sum(len(row['patterns']) for row in rows),
                'checker_sha256': hashlib.sha256((root / 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                'baseline_sha256': hashlib.sha256(data.encode()).hexdigest(),
                'patterns': dict(Counter(rule for row in rows for rule in row['patterns']))}
    (root / 'tools/ci_hygiene_baseline.meta.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    return metadata


def remap_baseline(root):
    rows = json.loads((root / BASELINE).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    mapping, unmapped = {}, []
    texts = {}
    for row in rows:
        try:
            name = row['file']
            if name not in texts:
                texts[name] = (root / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
            converted = normalise_baseline([row], texts.__getitem__)
            for key, patterns in converted.items():
                mapping.setdefault(key, set()).update(patterns)
        except (KeyError, ValueError, OSError) as error:
            unmapped.append({'entry': row, 'reason': str(error)})
    report = {'before_entries': len(rows), 'after_content_identities': len(mapping),
              'after_rule_bindings': sum(map(len, mapping.values())),
              'identity_count_difference': len(mapping) - len(rows), 'unmapped': unmapped,
              'combined_rule_identities': [dict(file=k[0], content_sha256=k[1], occurrence=k[2], patterns=sorted(v))
                                           for k, v in mapping.items() if len(v) > 1]}
    if not unmapped:
        report['metadata'] = write_baseline(root, mapping, 'python tools/check_ci_hygiene.py --remap-baseline')
    target = root / 'evidence/final-gate-probe-20261103/baseline-remap.json'
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    print(json.dumps(report))
    return int(bool(unmapped))


def emit_baseline(root):
    mapping = {}
    for path in sorted((root / '.github/workflows').glob('*.y*ml')):
        text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        locations = content_locations(text)
        for (name, line, rule), _, _ in issues(path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), text):
            digest, occurrence = locations[line]
            mapping.setdefault((name, digest, occurrence), set()).add(rule)
    print(json.dumps(write_baseline(root, mapping, 'python tools/check_ci_hygiene.py --emit-baseline')))
    return 0


def issues(path, text, selected=None, added=()):
    document = load(text)
    result = []
    locations = run_locations(text)
    for job, index, step, shell in steps(document):
        body = step['run']
        start = locations[job, index]
        key = (job, step.get('name', ''), body, shell, step.get('continue-on-error', 'false'))

        def error(rule, detail, expected, offset=0):
            if selected is None or rule == selected:
                message = f'[{rule}] expected={expected}; actual={detail}; candidate={path} jobs.{job}.steps[{index}]'
                result.append(((path, start + offset, rule), rule, f'{path}:{start + offset} {message}'))
                if rule in ('failfast', 'negative'):
                    for number in sorted(set(added) & set(range(start, start + len(body.splitlines())))):
                        if number != start + offset:
                            result.append(((path, number, rule), rule, f'{path}:{number} {message}'))

        if step.get('continue-on-error') == 'true':
            error('failfast', 'continue-on-error=true', 'unhandled failures stop the job')
        problem = shell_problem(shell, body)
        if problem:
            error('failfast', problem + f'; shell={shell or "runner default bash -e"}', 'Actions explicit bash, strict default-shell preamble, or PowerShell error propagation')
        commands, _ = command_shape(body)
        for command in commands:
            masked = re.sub(r"'(?:[^']*)'|\"(?:\\.|[^\"\\])*\"", ' ARG ', command)
            if re.search(r'\|\|\s*(?:true|:)\s*(?:$|;)|;\s*:\s*(?:$|;)', masked):
                error('failfast', 'failure swallowed by || true/: or ; :', 'preserve the first unhandled nonzero exit')
                break
        for offset, line in enumerate(body.splitlines()):
            if re.search(r'(?:^|;)\s*(?:test\s+-n\b|\[\s+-n\b)', line):
                if not ('||' in line and all(word in line.lower() for word in ('expected', 'actual', 'candidate'))):
                    error('diagnostic', line.strip(), 'nonempty check prints expected / actual / candidate paths (or ci_require_nonempty)', offset)
        if re.search(r'\bset\s+\+e\b', body):
            required = (r'rc=\$\?', r'set -e\b', r'\[\s*"\$rc"\s+-ne\s+0\s*\]\s*\|\|', 'control did not fail')
            if not all(re.search(pattern, body) for pattern in required):
                error('negative', 'incomplete expected-failure wrapper', 'set +e; command; rc=$?; set -e; [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] || fail')
            elif re.search(r'(?:>|&>)[^\n]*[/\\]dev[/\\]null', body) or ('2>&1' in body and not re.search(r'\bcat\b', body)):
                error('negative', 'rejected command diagnostics are hidden', 'print actual stdout/stderr of the rejected command')
        if re.search(r'^\s*(?:if\s+!\s+|!\s+)(?:cmake|ctest|python[0-9]*|\./)', body, re.M):
            error('negative', 'implicit expected failure loses the actual status', 'ci_expect_failure or explicit rc capture with visible error')
    if selected is None or selected == 'packaging':
        for job_id, job in document.get('jobs', {}).items():
            installed = set()
            for index, step in enumerate(job.get('steps', [])):
                body = step.get('run', '')
                # Match explicit SDK prefixes and the native workflow's two-target loop.
                for line in body.splitlines():
                    product = re.search(r'\btools/build_apple_product\.py\s+.*?--sdk\s+[\"\']?([^\s\"\']+)', line)
                    if product:
                        value = product[1]
                        installed.update((value.replace('$sdk', target) for target in ('iphoneos', 'iphonesimulator')) if '$sdk' in value else (value,))
                    host = re.search(r'\btools/build_apple_host\.py\s+.*?--sdk\s+[\"\']?([^\s\"\']+)', line)
                    if host and host[1] not in installed:
                        number = locations[job_id, index] + body.splitlines().index(line)
                        key = (path, number, 'packaging')
                        actual = ', '.join(sorted(installed)) or '<none>'
                        result.append((key, 'packaging', f'{path}:{number} [packaging] expected=product install before host for SDK {host[1]}; actual=installed prefixes {actual}; candidate={host[1]}/include/exreader-product and {host[1]}/lib in jobs.{job_id}.steps[{index}]'))
    return result


def added_lines(root, base):
    """New content occurrences only: moving an unchanged line is not new debt."""
    changed = set(subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'diff', '--no-ext-diff', '--no-renames', '--name-only', base, '--', '.github/workflows'], text=True).splitlines())
    tracked = set(subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'ls-files', '.github/workflows'], text=True).splitlines())
    changed.update(p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in (root / '.github/workflows').glob('*.y*ml') if p.relative_to(root).as_posix() not in tracked)
    result = {}
    for name in changed:
        path = root / name
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        old = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(root), 'show', f'{base}:{name}'], capture_output=True)
        previous = set(content_locations(old.stdout.decode('utf-8')).values()) if old.returncode == 0 else set()
        result[name] = {number for number, key in content_locations(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')).items() if key not in previous}
    return result


def line_sha(line):
    return hashlib.sha256(line.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()


def check(root, base=None, selected=None):
    errors, debt = 0, Counter()
    added = added_lines(root, base) if base else {}
    rows = json.loads((root / BASELINE).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    previous, frozen, base_texts = {}, False, {}
    if base:
        old = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(root), 'show', f'{base}:{BASELINE}'], capture_output=True)
        if old.returncode == 0:
            frozen = True
            def base_text(name):
                if name not in base_texts:
                    base_texts[name] = subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'show', f'{base}:{name}']).decode('utf-8')
                return base_texts[name]
            try:
                previous = normalise_baseline(json.loads(old.stdout), base_text)
            except (KeyError, ValueError, OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
                print(f'ERROR: {BASELINE}:1 expected=valid frozen base; actual={error}')
                return 1
    allowed, files, locations, declared = {}, {}, {}, set()
    for row in rows:
        name = row.get('file', '')
        reason, number = None, 1
        try:
            key = baseline_key(row)
            if key in declared:
                raise ValueError('duplicate content identity')
            declared.add(key)
            patterns = row['patterns']
            if not isinstance(patterns, list) or not patterns or len(set(patterns)) != len(patterns) or not set(patterns) <= set(RULES):
                raise ValueError('invalid rule obligations')
            if selected and selected not in patterns:
                continue
            if not name.startswith('.github/workflows/') or Path(name).is_absolute() or '..' in Path(name).parts or '\\' in name:
                raise ValueError('invalid exact baseline file')
            if not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', key[1]) or type(key[2]) is not int or key[2] < 1:
                raise ValueError('invalid content hash/occurrence')
            if key in allowed:
                raise ValueError('duplicate content identity')
            if name not in files:
                files[name] = (root / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                locations[name] = {value: line for line, value in content_locations(files[name]).items()}
            number = locations[name].get(key[1:], 1)
            if key[1:] not in locations[name]:
                raise ValueError('baseline content occurrence missing; remove stale entry')
            if frozen and (key not in previous or not set(patterns) <= previous[key]):
                raise ValueError('baseline may shrink only; new content/rule waiver rejected')
            allowed[key] = set(patterns) & ({selected} if selected else set(RULES))
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, OSError) as error:
            reason = str(error)
        if reason:
            print(f'ERROR: {name}:{number} expected=live exact content identity; actual={reason}; candidate={BASELINE}')
            errors += 1
    seen = set()
    for path in sorted((root / '.github/workflows').glob('*.y*ml')):
        name = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        identities = content_locations(text)
        for (file, number, rule), _, message in issues(name, text, selected, added.get(name, ())):
            key = (file, *identities[number])
            obligation = (key, rule)
            if obligation in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(obligation)
            if rule in allowed.get(key, set()):
                debt[rule] += 1
                print('EXISTING: ' + message)
            else:
                errors += 1
                print('ERROR: ' + message)
    for key, patterns in allowed.items():
        for rule in patterns:
            if (key, rule) not in seen:
                number = locations[key[0]][key[1:]]
                print(f'ERROR: {key[0]}:{number} [{rule}] expected=live violation; actual=no match (fixed/disappeared); candidate={BASELINE}; remove stale entry')
                errors += 1
    print(f'{"FAIL" if errors else "PASS"}: CI hygiene; violations={errors}; existing debt={dict(debt)}')
    return int(bool(errors))


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--base', help='PR base SHA for added-line detection (git diff -U0)')
    parser.add_argument('--rule', choices=RULES)
    parser.add_argument('--remap-baseline', action='store_true', help='Map existing debt only to content identities; do not admit new violations')
    parser.add_argument('--emit-baseline', action='store_true', help='Explicit owner-approved regeneration; never run automatically in CI')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.remap_baseline:
        return remap_baseline(ROOT)
    if args.emit_baseline:
        return emit_baseline(ROOT)
    if args.base:
        subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(ROOT), 'rev-parse', '--verify', args.base + '^{commit}'], check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    return check(ROOT, args.base, args.rule)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Small positive/rejection checks; synthetic validator fixtures, never SDK evidence."""
import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import sys
import tempfile
import tarfile

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'tools'))
from reading_release import check_profile, digest, seal


def write(path, value):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value) if isinstance(value, dict) else value, encoding='utf-8')


def fixture(root, profile):
    stage, reports = root / 'stage', root / 'reports'
    write(stage / 'include/exr/base.h', '#define EXR_VERSION "3.40.0"\n#define EXR_ABI_VERSION 1u\n')
    write(stage / 'LICENSE', 'synthetic material for validator self-check\n')
    write(stage / 'THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md', 'synthetic notices for validator self-check\n')
    library = profile == 'reading-library'
    if library:
        write(stage / 'include/exr/library.h', '#define EXR_LIBRARY_VERSION "3.34.0"\n')
    files = [{'path': p.relative_to(stage).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size,
              'sha256': digest(p)} for p in sorted(stage.rglob('*')) if p.is_file()]
    write(reports / 'profile.json', {'source_sha': 'a'*40, 'profile': profile,
                                   'accepted': True, 'problems': [], 'files': files})
    write(reports / 'licenses.json', {'source_sha': 'a'*40, 'profile': profile,
                                     'materials_check_passed': True, 'release_ready': True,
                                     'failures': [], 'pending': [], 'mandatory_missing': []})
    write(reports / 'corpus/summary.json', {'source_sha': 'a'*40, 'accepted': True, 'source_dirty': False,
                                         'identities_unchanged': True, 'results': [{} for _ in range(9)]})
    names = ['installed-core', 'installed-chapter', 'installed-convert']
    if library:
        names += ['installed-library', 'installed-library-source']
    write(reports / 'consumer-tests.xml', '<testsuite>' + ''.join('<testcase name="'+n+'"/>' for n in names) + '</testsuite>')
    runtime = root / 'runtime.json'
    write(runtime, {'sdk_version': '3.40.0', 'abi': 1, 'library_header_version': '3.34.0' if library else None})
    db = root / 'library.sqlite' if library else None
    if db:
        with sqlite3.connect(db) as connection:
            connection.execute('PRAGMA application_id=1163416131')
            connection.execute('PRAGMA user_version=7')
    return stage, reports, runtime, db


def main():
    cases = []
    for profile in ('reading-core', 'reading-library'):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            stage, reports, runtime, db = fixture(root, profile)
            check = lambda: check_profile(profile, 'a'*40, stage, reports, runtime, db)
            identity = check()
            ci = {'GITHUB_RUN_ID': '1', 'GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT': '1', 'RUNNER_OS': 'Linux', 'RUNNER_ARCH': 'X64'}
            first = seal(stage, root/'one', profile, 'a'*40, identity, ci, 1000, 'Linux', 'X64')
            second = seal(stage, root/'two', profile, 'a'*40, identity, ci, 1000, 'Linux', 'X64')
            assert first['sha256'] == second['sha256'] and first['quality_accepted'] is False
            cases.append(profile + ':positive-and-reproducible')
            composition = json.loads((reports/'profile.json').read_text())
            for host, arch in (('Windows', 'X64'), ('macOS', 'ARM64')):
                platform = dict(composition, host=host, platform_requested=host,
                                installed_files=composition['files'])
                platform.pop('files')
                write(reports/'platform-evidence.json', platform)
                platform_identity = check_profile(profile, 'a'*40, stage, reports, runtime, db, host)
                platform_ci = dict(ci, RUNNER_OS=host, RUNNER_ARCH=arch)
                candidate = seal(stage, root/host, profile, 'a'*40, platform_identity, platform_ci, 1000, host, arch)
                assert candidate['host'] == host and candidate['arch'] == arch and candidate['published'] is False
                assert candidate['archive'].endswith(f'-{host}-{arch}.tar.gz') and not candidate['quality_accepted']
                with tarfile.open(root/host/candidate['archive']) as archive:
                    manifests = [m for m in archive.getmembers() if m.name.endswith('/release-manifest.json')]
                    assert len(manifests) == 1
                    manifest = json.load(archive.extractfile(manifests[0]))
                    assert manifest['host'] == host and manifest['arch'] == arch and manifest['ci'] == platform_ci
                cases.append(profile + ':' + host + ':candidate-identity')
                for changes in ({'source_sha': 'b'*40}, {'accepted': False}, {'host': 'other'}, {'installed_files': []}):
                    write(reports/'platform-evidence.json', dict(platform, **changes))
                    try: check_profile(profile, 'a'*40, stage, reports, runtime, db, host)
                    except ValueError: cases.append(profile + ':' + host + ':refused-' + ','.join(changes))
                    else: raise AssertionError('accepted invalid platform composition')
                write(reports/'platform-evidence.json', platform)
            try: seal(stage, root/'wrong-host', profile, 'a'*40, identity, ci, 1000, 'Windows', 'X64')
            except ValueError: cases.append(profile + ':runner-mismatch')
            else: raise AssertionError('accepted mismatched runner identity')
            mutations = [
                ('profile.json', {'source_sha': 'b'*40}),
                ('profile.json', {'accepted': False}),
                ('profile.json', {'profile': 'unknown'}),
                ('profile.json', {'files': []}),
                ('licenses.json', {'release_ready': False}),
                ('licenses.json', {'pending': ['missing notice']}),
                ('licenses.json', {'mandatory_missing': ['missing notice']}),
                ('corpus/summary.json', {'accepted': False}),
                ('corpus/summary.json', {'source_dirty': True}),
                ('corpus/summary.json', {'identities_unchanged': False}),
                ('corpus/summary.json', {'results': []}),
            ]
            for name, changes in mutations:
                path = reports/name
                original = json.loads(path.read_text())
                altered = copy.deepcopy(original); altered.update(changes); write(path, altered)
                try:
                    check()
                except ValueError:
                    cases.append(profile + ':' + name + ':' + ','.join(changes))
                else:
                    raise AssertionError('accepted invalid report: ' + name)
                finally:
                    write(path, original)
            xml = reports/'consumer-tests.xml'
            original = xml.read_text()
            for text in (original.replace('<testcase name="installed-core"/>', ''),
                         original.replace('<testcase name="installed-core"/>', '<testcase name="installed-core"><skipped/></testcase>')):
                write(xml, text)
                try: check()
                except ValueError: cases.append(profile + ':missing-or-skipped-consumer')
                else: raise AssertionError('accepted incomplete installed consumer')
            write(xml, original)
            original = runtime.read_text(); write(runtime, {'sdk_version': '3.40.0', 'abi': 2})
            try: check()
            except ValueError: cases.append(profile + ':runtime-abi')
            else: raise AssertionError('accepted wrong runtime ABI')
            write(runtime, original)
            write(stage/'LICENSE', 'altered installed material')
            try: check()
            except ValueError: cases.append(profile + ':changed-stage')
            else: raise AssertionError('accepted changed installed stage')
    print(json.dumps({'accepted': True, 'checks': len(cases), 'cases': cases}))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION ${CMAKE_VERSION}) # this file comes with cmake

function(check_file_hash has_hash hash_is_good)
  if("${has_hash}" STREQUAL "")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "has_hash Can't be empty")
  endif()

  if("${hash_is_good}" STREQUAL "")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "hash_is_good Can't be empty")
  endif()

  if("SHA256" STREQUAL "")
    # No check
    set("${has_hash}" FALSE PARENT_SCOPE)
    set("${hash_is_good}" FALSE PARENT_SCOPE)
    return()
  endif()

  set("${has_hash}" TRUE PARENT_SCOPE)

  message(VERBOSE "verifying file...
       file='C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar'")

  file("SHA256" "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar" actual_value)

  if(NOT "${actual_value}" STREQUAL "87c7f1f8699b91e89f773f7dcfa505b044e1dae67f62a55d4e29c3afdcac4fb8")
    set("${hash_is_good}" FALSE PARENT_SCOPE)
    message(VERBOSE "SHA256 hash of
    C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar
  does not match expected value
    expected: '87c7f1f8699b91e89f773f7dcfa505b044e1dae67f62a55d4e29c3afdcac4fb8'
      actual: '${actual_value}'")
  else()
    set("${hash_is_good}" TRUE PARENT_SCOPE)
  endif()
endfunction()

function(sleep_before_download attempt)
  if(attempt EQUAL 0)
    return()
  endif()

  if(attempt EQUAL 1)
    message(VERBOSE "Retrying...")
    return()
  endif()

  set(sleep_seconds 0)

  if(attempt EQUAL 2)
    set(sleep_seconds 5)
  elseif(attempt EQUAL 3)
    set(sleep_seconds 5)
  elseif(attempt EQUAL 4)
    set(sleep_seconds 15)
  elseif(attempt EQUAL 5)
    set(sleep_seconds 60)
  elseif(attempt EQUAL 6)
    set(sleep_seconds 90)
  elseif(attempt EQUAL 7)
    set(sleep_seconds 300)
  else()
    set(sleep_seconds 1200)
  endif()

  message(VERBOSE "Retry after ${sleep_seconds} seconds (attempt #${attempt}) ...")

  execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -E sleep "${sleep_seconds}")
endfunction()

if(EXISTS "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar")
  check_file_hash(has_hash hash_is_good)
  if(has_hash)
    if(hash_is_good)
      message(VERBOSE "File already exists and hash match (skip download):
  file='C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar'
  SHA256='87c7f1f8699b91e89f773f7dcfa505b044e1dae67f62a55d4e29c3afdcac4fb8'"
      )
      return()
    else()
      message(VERBOSE "File already exists but hash mismatch. Removing...")
      file(REMOVE "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar")
    endif()
  else()
    message(VERBOSE "File already exists but no hash specified (use URL_HASH):
  file='C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar'
Old file will be removed and new file downloaded from URL."
    )
    file(REMOVE "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar")
  endif()
endif()

set(retry_number 5)

message(VERBOSE "Downloading...
   dst='C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar'
   timeout='none'
   inactivity timeout='none'"
)
set(download_retry_codes 7 6 8 15 28 35)
set(skip_url_list)
set(status_code)
foreach(i RANGE ${retry_number})
  if(status_code IN_LIST download_retry_codes)
    sleep_before_download(${i})
  endif()
  foreach(url IN ITEMS [====[https://codeload.github.com/freetype/freetype/tar.gz/refs/tags/VER-2-15-0]====])
    if(NOT url IN_LIST skip_url_list)
      message(VERBOSE "Using src='${url}'")

      
      
      set(CMAKE_TLS_CAINFO "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/cf112-windows-ca.pem")
      
      

      file(
        DOWNLOAD
        "${url}" "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar"
        SHOW_PROGRESS
        # no TIMEOUT
        # no INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT
        STATUS status
        LOG log
        
        
        )

      list(GET status 0 status_code)
      list(GET status 1 status_string)

      if(status_code EQUAL 0)
        check_file_hash(has_hash hash_is_good)
        if(has_hash AND NOT hash_is_good)
          message(VERBOSE "Hash mismatch, removing...")
          file(REMOVE "C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf112/_deps/freetype-subbuild/freetype-populate-prefix/src/archive.tar")
        else()
          message(VERBOSE "Downloading... done")
          return()
        endif()
      else()
        string(APPEND logFailedURLs "error: downloading '${url}' failed
        status_code: ${status_code}
        status_string: ${status_string}
        log:
        --- LOG BEGIN ---
        ${log}
        --- LOG END ---
        "
        )
      if(NOT status_code IN_LIST download_retry_codes)
        list(APPEND skip_url_list "${url}")
        break()
      endif()
    endif()
  endif()
  endforeach()
endforeach()

message(FATAL_ERROR "Each download failed!
  ${logFailedURLs}
  "
)

+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:

+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)

+    for stage in [2, 3]:

+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())

      require_budgets: true

    name: reading-required
    if: always()
    needs: [protocol, native, profiles, platforms, corpus, performance]
    runs-on: ubuntu-22.04
    timeout-minutes: 20
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

          fetch-depth: 0

          sudo apt-get update -qq
          sudo apt-get install -y python3-pil
      - name: Preserve every upstream result without skipping the final check
        env:
          UPSTREAM_RESULTS: ${{ toJSON(needs) }}
        run: |
          mkdir -p build/reading-required
          printf '%s\n' "$UPSTREAM_RESULTS" > build/reading-required/needs.json
      - name: Read same-run immutable artifact metadata and download in cloud only
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          path: build/reading-required/artifacts
          pattern: '{reading-*,r2-real-corpus-*}'
      - name: Strict same-source, same-attempt quality and release-material summary
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: python3 tools/reading_required.py build/reading-required/artifacts build/reading-required/needs.json build/reading-required/summary.json
      - name: Preserve rejection or acceptance without publishing
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: reading-required-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
          path: |
            build/reading-required/needs.json
            build/reading-required/summary.json

+        for q in [p, *p.children(recursive=True)]:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, ssl
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+if (repo / '.git' / 'MERGE_HEAD').exists() or subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', '-q', '--verify', 'MERGE_HEAD'], cwd=repo, capture_output=True).returncode == 0:
+    subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', 'merge: integrate current main into PR 112 preserving both reports'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+certfile = repo.parent / 'cf112-windows-ca.pem'
+certfile.write_text(''.join(ssl.DER_cert_to_PEM_cert(cert) for store in ['ROOT', 'CA'] for cert, encoding, trust in ssl.enum_certificates(store) if encoding == 'x509_asn'), encoding='ascii')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 3: configure environment]\nOriginal configure failed: bundled CMake TLS has no trust anchors. Exported trusted Windows certificate stores to an external build CA bundle and supplied CMAKE_TLS_CAINFO (no system settings changed). Previous source overrides point to removed exr-build-argon caches; cleared these overrides. Re-enabled AAC previously disabled by the interrupted run; use default feature coverage and pinned hashes. Protected files byte-identical to HEAD and origin/main; two incoming src headers registered in tools/layers.json.\n')
+args = ['cmake', '-S', str(repo), '-B', str(build), '-G', 'Ninja', '-DEXR_READER_WINDOWS_SHELL=ON', '-DEXR_ENABLE_AAC=ON', '-DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_MBEDTLS=C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-deps/mbedtls-3.6.7', f'-DCMAKE_TLS_CAINFO={certfile.as_posix()}']
+with (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    result = subprocess.run(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+print('Configure exit:', result.returncode, flush=True)
+print((repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')[-18000:])

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)
+cache = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\exr-build-cf112\CMakeCache.txt')
+for line in cache.read_text().splitlines():
+    if line.startswith(('EXR_ENABLE_AAC:', 'FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_', 'CMAKE_C_COMPILER:', 'CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER:')):
+        print(line)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)

+certfile = repo.parent / 'cf112-windows-ca.pem'

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)
+cache = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\exr-build-cf112\CMakeCache.txt')
+for line in cache.read_text().splitlines():
+    if line.startswith(('EXR_ENABLE_AAC:', 'FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_', 'CMAKE_C_COMPILER:', 'CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER:')):
+        print(line)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)

# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# Independent consumer build for the Windows reference host.
#
# examples/CMakeLists.txt is owned by the integration branch, so this project
# consumes the installed SDK directly and reuses ../reader.c without touching
# the shared example entry point. It also works from the installed tree
# (stage/examples/windows).
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
project(EXReaderWindowsHost LANGUAGES C)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 11)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
if(NOT EXR_SDK)
  message(FATAL_ERROR "Set -DEXR_SDK=<installed SDK prefix>")
endif()
if(MSVC)
  set(CMAKE_MSVC_RUNTIME_LIBRARY "MultiThreaded$<$<CONFIG:Debug>:Debug>")
  add_compile_options(/utf-8)
endif()
include("${EXR_SDK}/lib/cmake/EXReader/EXReaderConfigVersion.cmake")
find_package(EXReader ${PACKAGE_VERSION} EXACT CONFIG REQUIRED PATHS "${EXR_SDK}" NO_DEFAULT_PATH)
add_executable(EXReader WIN32 ../reader.c)
option(EXR_READER_TEST_DRIVERS "Compile acceptance commands (test builds only)" OFF)
if(EXR_READER_TEST_DRIVERS)
  target_compile_definitions(EXReader PRIVATE EXR_READER_TEST_DRIVERS=1)
endif()
target_link_libraries(EXReader PRIVATE EXReader::exreader comdlg32 gdi32 uiautomationcore shell32 user32)
if(NOT MSVC)
  target_link_libraries(EXReader PRIVATE m)
endif()
set(EXR_READER_WINDOWS_SHELL ON) # The retired native toolbar has no build path.
if(EXR_READER_WINDOWS_SHELL)
  enable_language(CXX)
  target_sources(EXReader PRIVATE shell_d2d.cpp shell_epub.cpp shell_speech.cpp)
  target_link_libraries(EXReader PRIVATE ole32 uuid)
  # The same warning gate as the standalone exreader-shell-d2d library, on this
  # one file only: the C sources of EXReader keep their own flags.
  if(MSVC)
    set_source_files_properties(shell_d2d.cpp PROPERTIES COMPILE_OPTIONS "/W4;/WX")
  else()
    set_source_files_properties(shell_d2d.cpp PROPERTIES COMPILE_OPTIONS "-Wall;-Wextra;-Werror")
  endif()
  target_compile_features(EXReader PRIVATE cxx_std_17)
  target_compile_definitions(EXReader PRIVATE EXR_READER_WINDOWS_SHELL=1)
  target_link_libraries(EXReader PRIVATE d2d1 dwrite advapi32 EXReader::vendor_miniz EXReader::vendor_expat)
  add_executable(check_shell_epub EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL check_shell_epub.cpp)
  target_compile_features(check_shell_epub PRIVATE cxx_std_17)
  target_link_libraries(check_shell_epub PRIVATE EXReader::exreader EXReader::vendor_miniz EXReader::vendor_expat)
  add_executable(check_shell_geometry EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL check_shell_geometry.c)
  target_link_libraries(check_shell_geometry PRIVATE EXReader::exreader)
  if(MSVC)
    target_compile_options(check_shell_geometry PRIVATE /UNDEBUG)
  else()
    target_compile_options(check_shell_geometry PRIVATE -UNDEBUG)
    target_link_libraries(check_shell_geometry PRIVATE m)
  endif()
  add_executable(check_shell_anchor EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL check_shell_anchor.c shell_d2d.cpp shell_epub.cpp shell_speech.cpp)
  target_link_libraries(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE ole32 uuid)
  target_compile_features(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE cxx_std_17)
  target_compile_definitions(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE EXR_READER_WINDOWS_SHELL=1)
  target_link_libraries(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE EXReader::exreader comdlg32 gdi32 uiautomationcore shell32 user32 d2d1 dwrite advapi32 EXReader::vendor_miniz EXReader::vendor_expat)
  if(MSVC)
    target_compile_options(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE /UNDEBUG)
  else()
    target_compile_options(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE -UNDEBUG)
    target_link_options(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE -static)
    target_link_libraries(check_shell_anchor PRIVATE m)
  endif()
  if(NOT MSVC)
    target_link_options(EXReader PRIVATE -static)
  endif()
endif()
get_target_property(exr_type EXReader::exreader TYPE)
if(exr_type STREQUAL "SHARED_LIBRARY")
  find_file(EXR_RUNTIME NAMES exreader.dll libexreader.dll
            PATHS "${EXR_SDK}/bin" NO_DEFAULT_PATH REQUIRED)

    COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E copy_if_different "${EXR_RUNTIME}" "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:EXReader>"

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)
+cache = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\exr-build-cf112\CMakeCache.txt')
+for line in cache.read_text().splitlines():
+    if line.startswith(('EXR_ENABLE_AAC:', 'FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_', 'CMAKE_C_COMPILER:', 'CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER:')):
+        print(line)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)
+cache = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\exr-build-cf112\CMakeCache.txt')
+for line in cache.read_text().splitlines():
+    if line.startswith(('EXR_ENABLE_AAC:', 'FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_', 'CMAKE_C_COMPILER:', 'CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER:')):
+        print(line)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+import psutil, time
+from pathlib import Path
+for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name']):
+    if p.info['name'].lower() == 'cmake.exe':
+        try:
+            if 'exr-build-cf112' not in ' '.join(p.cmdline()) or '--build' not in p.cmdline():
+                continue
+            for q in [p, *p.children(recursive=True)]:
+                print(q.pid, q.name(), 'age', round(time.time()-q.create_time()), 'cpu', q.cpu_times()[:2], 'MiB', q.memory_info().rss//1048576, 'cmd', ' '.join(q.cmdline())[:700], flush=True)
+        except psutil.NoSuchProcess:
+            pass

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, ssl

+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+if (repo / '.git' / 'MERGE_HEAD').exists() or subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', '-q', '--verify', 'MERGE_HEAD'], cwd=repo, capture_output=True).returncode == 0:
+    subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', 'merge: integrate current main into PR 112 preserving both reports'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+certfile = repo.parent / 'cf112-windows-ca.pem'
+certfile.write_text(''.join(ssl.DER_cert_to_PEM_cert(cert) for store in ['ROOT', 'CA'] for cert, encoding, trust in ssl.enum_certificates(store) if encoding == 'x509_asn'), encoding='ascii')

Exported trusted Windows certificate stores to an external build CA bundle and supplied CMAKE_TLS_CAINFO (no system settings changed). +from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+branch = 'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+assert git('branch', '--show-current').decode().strip() == branch
+assert not git('ls-files', '-u')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', 'origin/main', 'HEAD'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+ctest = (repo.parent / 'cf112-ctest.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+match = re.search(r'(\d+)% tests passed, (\d+) tests failed out of (\d+)', ctest)
+assert match and match[1] == '100' and match[2] == '0', 'CTest must pass before push'
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    assert data == git('show', f'8d34bbc6:{name}'), name
+    assert data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'), name
+assert hashlib.sha256((repo / 'tests/check_reading_release.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest() == '6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b'
+changed = git('diff', '--name-only').decode().splitlines()
+assert changed == ['OUT-CF.txt'], changed
+assert not git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only')
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt', 'RESOLVE.md']:
+    assert not re.search(rb'^(?:<<<<<<< |=======$|>>>>>>> )', (repo / name).read_bytes(), re.M), name
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write(f'\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 5: pre-push verification]\nFull requested build succeeded. CTest: {match[3]}/{match[3]} passed; failed tests: none; only sdk-release excluded as requested. Re-fetched origin/main and verified it is fully contained in HEAD (0 incoming commits). No unmerged paths or conflict markers in resolved reports. Three protected files remain byte-identical to recovery HEAD 8d34bbc6 and origin/main; reading-release SHA256 remains 6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b. Only OUT-CF.txt changed after validation. Committing results and pushing directly to {branch}.\n')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'add', '--', 'OUT-CF.txt'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+assert git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only').decode().splitlines() == ['OUT-CF.txt']
+subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', 'docs: record PR 112 merge validation and preserved constraints'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+subprocess.run(['git', 'push', 'origin', f'HEAD:{branch}'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+head = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD').decode().strip()
+result = {'head': head, 'branch': branch, 'ctest_passed': int(match[3]), 'ctest_total': int(match[3]), 'ctest_failed': [], 'excluded': ['sdk-release'], 'main': git('rev-parse', 'origin/main').decode().strip()}
+(repo.parent / 'cf112-pushed.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)
