#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Check complete traversal, caps and evidence rejection using real SDK calls."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).absolute().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'tools'))
from check_corpus import digest, valid_report
from run_benchmarks import make_epub


def main():
    binary, fixtures = (Path(p).absolute() for p in sys.argv[1:])
    font = fixtures/'fonts/primary.ttf'
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='exreader-complete-') as directory:
        root = Path(directory)
        book = root/'??.epub'
        make_epub(book, 3, 16384)

        def probe(path, limit=0):
            p = subprocess.run([str(binary), str(path), str(font), str(limit)],
                               capture_output=True, timeout=30)
            report = json.loads(p.stdout)
            assert valid_report(report)
            return p.returncode, report

        code, full = probe(book)
        assert code == 0 and full['complete'] and full['completed_parts'] == full['parts'] == 3
        assert full['pages'] > 3 and full['text_bytes'] >= 3*16384
        for command in ([str(binary), '--strict', str(book), str(font)],
                        [str(binary), str(book), str(font), '--strict']):
            checked = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, timeout=30)
            assert checked.returncode == 0 and json.loads(checked.stdout) == full
        wrong_mime = root/'????.epub'
        with zipfile.ZipFile(book) as source, zipfile.ZipFile(wrong_mime, 'w') as target:
            for entry in source.infolist():
                target.writestr(entry, b'application/not-epub' if entry.filename == 'mimetype'
                                else source.read(entry))
        code, tolerant = probe(wrong_mime)
        assert code == 0 and tolerant == full
        checked = subprocess.run([str(binary), str(wrong_mime), str(font), '--strict'],
                                 capture_output=True, timeout=30)
        strict = json.loads(checked.stdout)
        assert checked.returncode == 1 and strict['stage'] == 'validate' and strict['status']
        assert all(word in checked.stderr for word in (b'mimetype', b'expected', b'found'))
        code, limited = probe(book, 1)
        assert code == 1 and limited['status'] == 0 and not limited['complete'] and limited['pages'] == 1
        code, exact = probe(book, full['pages'])
        assert code == 0 and exact == full
        pdf = root/'sample.PdF'
        shutil.copyfile(fixtures/'pdf-color/shading-axial.pdf', pdf)
        code, fixed = probe(pdf)
        assert code == 0 and fixed['complete'] and fixed['pages'] == 1
        for name in ('sample.txt', 'comic.cbz'):
            code, report = probe(fixtures/name)
            assert code == 0 and report['complete'] and report['pages'] > 0
        bad = root/'bad.epub'
        bad.write_bytes(b'not a publication')
        code, rejected = probe(bad)
        assert code == 1 and rejected['status'] and not rejected['complete']
        for limit in ('-1', '+1', ' 1', '1x', '18446744073709551616'):
            p = subprocess.run([str(binary), str(book), str(font), limit], capture_output=True, timeout=30)
            assert p.returncode == 2 and not p.stdout
        manifest = root/'manifest.json'
        records = [{'path': p.name, 'sha256': digest(p), 'expected': {'status': 0, 'complete': True}}
                   for p in (book, pdf)]

        def corpus(name, expected, *options):
            p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/'tools/check_corpus.py'), str(binary), str(font),
                                str(manifest), str(root/name), *options], capture_output=True, timeout=60)
            assert p.returncode == (0 if expected else 1), name
            summary = json.loads((root/name/'summary.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            assert summary['accepted'] is expected
            assert all(digest(Path(r['result'])) == r['sha256'] for r in summary['results'])
            return summary

        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('full', True)['counts'] == {'passed': 2}
        assert corpus('parallel', True, '--workers', '2')['counts'] == {'passed': 2}
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps({'files': records, 'complete': False, 'target': 500}), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('collection-incomplete', False)['completed_documents'] == 2
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps({'files': records, 'complete': True, 'target': 500}), encoding='utf-8')
        corpus('collection-count-mismatch', False)
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert 'incomplete' in corpus('cap', False, '--max-pages', '1')['counts']
        corpus('subset', False, '--max-files', '1')
        records[0]['sha256'] = '0'*64
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('hash', False)['counts']['invalid_input'] == 1
        records[0]['sha256'] = digest(book)
        del records[0]['expected']
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('unknown', False)['counts']['untriaged'] == 1
        records[0] = {'path': bad.name, 'sha256': digest(bad),
                      'expected': {'status': 0, 'complete': True}}
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('unexpected', False)['counts']['unexpected_outcome'] == 1
        records[0]['expected'] = {'status': rejected['status'], 'complete': False}
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('reason-required', False)['counts']['invalid_expectation'] == 1
        records[0]['expectation_reason'] = 'Deliberately invalid test document'
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('known-rejection', True)['counts']['expected_rejection'] == 1
        records.append(records[1])
        manifest.write_text(json.dumps(records), encoding='utf-8')
        assert corpus('duplicate', False, '--workers', '2')['counts']['invalid_input'] == 1
    print('Complete-document probe and corpus evidence checks passed')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Real-process classification checks, including optional independent PDF checks."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).absolute().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'tools'))
from check_corpus import digest
from check_pdf_reference import valid_reference
from triage_corpus import category, limit_kind


def check(binary, fixtures, output):
    font = fixtures/'fonts/primary.ttf'
    bad = output/'invalid.epub'
    bad.write_bytes(b'not a publication')
    manifest = output/'manifest.json'
    manifest.write_text(json.dumps([{'path': bad.name, 'sha256': digest(bad),
        'expected': {'status': 0, 'complete': True}}]), encoding='utf-8')

    def run(script, args, name, expected):
        with (output/(name+'.stdout')).open('wb') as stdout, (output/(name+'.stderr')).open('wb') as stderr:
            p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT/script), *map(str, args)],
                               stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, timeout=60)
        assert p.returncode == expected, name

    baseline = output/'baseline'
    run('tools/check_corpus.py', [binary, font, manifest, baseline], 'baseline', 1)
    summary = baseline/'summary.json'
    args = [summary, binary, font, output/'classification', '--root', output]
    run('tools/triage_corpus.py', args, 'triage', 0)
    report = json.loads((output/'classification/classification.json').read_text())
    assert not report['accepted'] and not report['baseline_accepted'] and report['baseline_unchanged']
    assert report['counts'] == {'requires_review': 1} and report['replayed'] == 1
    assert category(5, {'complete': True, 'repaired': False}) == 'reference_completed_primary_rejected'
    assert category(5, {'complete': True, 'repaired': True}) == 'reference_repaired_input'
    assert category(5, {'complete': False, 'password_required': True}) == 'reference_requires_password'
    assert category(6, None) == 'resource_limit' and category(11, None) == 'unsupported_feature'
    assert limit_kind('Unbreakable content exceeds page height') == 'unbreakable_page_height'
    assert limit_kind('Table layout limit exceeded') == 'table_layout_budget'
    assert limit_kind('unknown limit') == 'other_budget'
    assert limit_kind('Decoded image cache exceeds 64 MiB') == 'decoded_image_cache'

    # A retained result hash alone must not authorize changed diagnostic logs.
    attachment = baseline/'00000/stderr.log'
    saved = attachment.read_bytes()
    attachment.write_bytes(saved+b'changed')
    args[3] = output/'attachment-changed'
    run('tools/triage_corpus.py', args, 'attachment-changed', 1)
    assert not (args[3]/'classification.json').exists()

    good = output/'valid.txt'
    good.write_text('A complete document.', encoding='utf-8')
    manifest.write_text(json.dumps([{'path': good.name, 'sha256': digest(good),
        'expected': {'status': 0, 'complete': True}}]), encoding='utf-8')
    run('tools/check_corpus.py', [binary, font, manifest, output/'good-baseline'], 'good-baseline', 0)
    args[0], args[3] = output/'good-baseline/summary.json', output/'good-classification'
    run('tools/triage_corpus.py', args, 'good-triage', 0)
    good_report = json.loads((args[3]/'classification.json').read_text())
    assert good_report['baseline_accepted'] and not good_report['accepted'] and not good_report['replayed']
    assert good_report['counts'] == {'baseline_completed': 1} and good_report['source_tools_unchanged']
    # A completion record with an inconsistent process exit cannot count as completed.
    operational = output/'operational'
    operational.mkdir()
    original = json.loads((output/'good-baseline/00000/result.json').read_text())
    for name in original['evidence']:
        shutil.copyfile(output/'good-baseline/00000'/name, operational/name)
    original['classification'], original['exit_code'] = 'process_failure', 1
    result_path = operational/'result.json'
    result_path.write_text(json.dumps(original), encoding='utf-8')
    synthetic = json.loads((output/'good-baseline/summary.json').read_text())
    synthetic['accepted'] = False
    synthetic['results'] = [{'result': str(result_path), 'sha256': digest(result_path)}]
    synthetic_path = operational/'summary.json'
    synthetic_path.write_text(json.dumps(synthetic), encoding='utf-8')
    args[0], args[3] = synthetic_path, output/'operational-triage'
    run('tools/triage_corpus.py', args, 'operational-triage', 0)
    diagnostic = json.loads((args[3]/'classification.json').read_text())
    assert diagnostic['counts'] == {'baseline_failure_not_reproduced': 1} and diagnostic['replayed'] == 1
    assert diagnostic['results'][0]['baseline_classification'] == 'process_failure' and not diagnostic['accepted']
    attachment.write_bytes(saved)
    bad.write_bytes(b'changed input')
    args[0], args[3] = summary, output/'input-changed'
    run('tools/triage_corpus.py', args, 'input-changed', 1)
    assert not (args[3]/'classification.json').exists()

    reference_checked = False
    if importlib.util.find_spec('pymupdf'):
        import pymupdf
        runtime_args = [output/'good-baseline/summary.json', binary, font, output/'reference-runtime-ledger',
                        '--root', output, '--pdf-reference']
        run('tools/triage_corpus.py', runtime_args, 'reference-runtime-ledger', 0)
        identity = json.loads((output/'reference-runtime-ledger/classification.json').read_text())['reference_identity']
        native = {str(p): digest(p) for pattern in ('*.pyd', '*.so*', '*.dll', '*.dylib')
                  for p in Path(pymupdf.__file__).parent.glob(pattern)}
        assert native and identity['native_modules'] == native
        with pymupdf.open() as document:
            document.new_page().insert_text((40, 40), 'Independent reference check')
            good = document.tobytes()
            protected = document.tobytes(encryption=pymupdf.PDF_ENCRYPT_AES_256,
                                         owner_pw='local-check-owner', user_pw='local-check-user')
        cases = [('valid', good, True, False, False),
                 ('repair', good[:good.rindex(b'startxref')], True, True, False),
                 ('rejected', b'not a document', False, False, False),
                 ('password', protected, False, False, True)]
        for name, content, complete, repaired, password in cases:
            path = output/(name+'.pdf')
            path.write_bytes(content)
            run('tools/check_pdf_reference.py', [path], name, 0 if complete else 1)
            result = json.loads((output/(name+'.stdout')).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            assert valid_reference(result), name
            assert (result['complete'], result['repaired'], result['password_required']) == (complete, repaired, password), name
            if complete:
                result['pages'] += 1
                assert not valid_reference(result)
        reference_checked = True
    (output/'checks.json').write_text(json.dumps({'passed': True, 'reference_checked': reference_checked,
        'scope': 'classification integrity and isolated reference behavior; no corpus acceptance'}, indent=2)+'\n')
    print('Corpus classification checks passed; independent PDF reference '+('checked' if reference_checked else 'unavailable'))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    binary, fixtures = (Path(p).absolute() for p in sys.argv[1:3])
    if len(sys.argv) == 4:
        output = Path(sys.argv[3]).absolute()
        output.mkdir()  # Preserve any previous evidence.
        check(binary, fixtures, output)
    else:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='exr-triage-') as directory:
            check(binary, fixtures, Path(directory))

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+branch = 'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+assert git('branch', '--show-current').decode().strip() == branch
+assert not git('ls-files', '-u')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', 'origin/main', 'HEAD'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+ctest = (repo.parent / 'cf112-ctest.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+match = re.search(r'(\d+)% tests passed, (\d+) tests failed out of (\d+)', ctest)
+assert match and match[1] == '100' and match[2] == '0', 'CTest must pass before push'
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    assert data == git('show', f'8d34bbc6:{name}'), name
+    assert data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'), name
+assert hashlib.sha256((repo / 'tests/check_reading_release.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest() == '6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b'
+changed = git('diff', '--name-only').decode().splitlines()
+assert changed == ['OUT-CF.txt'], changed
+assert not git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only')
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt', 'RESOLVE.md']:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+            pass

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+branch = 'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+assert git('branch', '--show-current').decode().strip() == branch
+assert not git('ls-files', '-u')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', 'origin/main', 'HEAD'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+ctest = (repo.parent / 'cf112-ctest.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+match = re.search(r'(\d+)% tests passed, (\d+) tests failed out of (\d+)', ctest)
+assert match and match[1] == '100' and match[2] == '0', 'CTest must pass before push'
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    assert data == git('show', f'8d34bbc6:{name}'), name
+    assert data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'), name
+assert hashlib.sha256((repo / 'tests/check_reading_release.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest() == '6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b'
+changed = git('diff', '--name-only').decode().splitlines()
+assert changed == ['OUT-CF.txt'], changed
+assert not git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only')
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt', 'RESOLVE.md']:
+    assert not re.search(rb'^(?:<<<<<<< |=======$|>>>>>>> )', (repo / name).read_bytes(), re.M), name
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write(f'\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 5: pre-push verification]\nFull requested build succeeded. CTest: {match[3]}/{match[3]} passed; failed tests: none; only sdk-release excluded as requested. Re-fetched origin/main and verified it is fully contained in HEAD (0 incoming commits). No unmerged paths or conflict markers in resolved reports. Three protected files remain byte-identical to recovery HEAD 8d34bbc6 and origin/main; reading-release SHA256 remains 6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b. Only OUT-CF.txt changed after validation. Committing results and pushing directly to {branch}.\n')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'add', '--', 'OUT-CF.txt'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+assert git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only').decode().splitlines() == ['OUT-CF.txt']
+subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', 'docs: record PR 112 merge validation and preserved constraints'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+subprocess.run(['git', 'push', 'origin', f'HEAD:{branch}'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+head = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD').decode().strip()

+            cmd = p.cmdline()
+            if 'exr-build-cf112' in ' '.join(cmd):
+                print('Owned probe process still alive:', p.pid, 'age', time.time()-p.create_time(), 'CPU', p.cpu_times(), cmd, flush=True)
+        except psutil.NoSuchProcess:
+            pass
+with (repo/'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 6: full CTest result and retry]\nInitial requested suite: 402/404 passed, 2 failed (native-document-probe, native-corpus-triage), total 2949.67s. Both failed with subprocess.TimeoutExpired at 60 seconds in tools/check_corpus.py invocations; document-probe cleanup additionally hit WinError 32 on a child-held stderr.log. No functional assertion failed before these timeouts. native-batch, native-bench-batch-statistics, and all DOCX real-world regressions passed. Preserved full first-run detail in cf112-ctest-first-detail.log. Starting ctest --rerun-failed --output-on-failure -j 1 with unchanged source, binaries, assertions and timeouts.\n')
+log = base/'cf112-retry.log'
+print('Starting serial retry; log:', log, flush=True)
+with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    p = subprocess.run(['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '1', '--rerun-failed', '--output-on-failure'], cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)

+print(data[-18000:], flush=True)
+with (repo/'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path

+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')

+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:

+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())

+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))

+serialized = json.dumps(layers)

+for header in headers:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+branch = 'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+assert git('branch', '--show-current').decode().strip() == branch
+assert not git('ls-files', '-u')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', 'origin/main', 'HEAD'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+ctest = (repo.parent / 'cf112-ctest.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+match = re.search(r'(\d+)% tests passed, (\d+) tests failed out of (\d+)', ctest)
+assert match and match[1] == '100' and match[2] == '0', 'CTest must pass before push'
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    assert data == git('show', f'8d34bbc6:{name}'), name
+    assert data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'), name
+assert hashlib.sha256((repo / 'tests/check_reading_release.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest() == '6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b'
+changed = git('diff', '--name-only').decode().splitlines()
+assert changed == ['OUT-CF.txt'], changed
+assert not git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only')
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt', 'RESOLVE.md']:
+    assert not re.search(rb'^(?:<<<<<<< |=======$|>>>>>>> )', (repo / name).read_bytes(), re.M), name
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write(f'\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 5: pre-push verification]\nFull requested build succeeded. CTest: {match[3]}/{match[3]} passed; failed tests: none; only sdk-release excluded as requested. Re-fetched origin/main and verified it is fully contained in HEAD (0 incoming commits). No unmerged paths or conflict markers in resolved reports. Three protected files remain byte-identical to recovery HEAD 8d34bbc6 and origin/main; reading-release SHA256 remains 6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b. Only OUT-CF.txt changed after validation. Committing results and pushing directly to {branch}.\n')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'add', '--', 'OUT-CF.txt'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+assert git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only').decode().splitlines() == ['OUT-CF.txt']
+subprocess.run(['git', 'commit', '-m', 'docs: record PR 112 merge validation and preserved constraints'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+subprocess.run(['git', 'push', 'origin', f'HEAD:{branch}'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+head = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD').decode().strip()

+from pathlib import Path

+    if p.info['name'].lower() == 'exreader-check-document.exe':
+        try:
+            cmd = p.cmdline()
+            if 'exr-build-cf112' in ' '.join(cmd):
+                print('Owned probe process still alive:', p.pid, 'age', time.time()-p.create_time(), 'CPU', p.cpu_times(), cmd, flush=True)
+        except psutil.NoSuchProcess:
+            pass
+with (repo/'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 6: full CTest result and retry]\nInitial requested suite: 402/404 passed, 2 failed (native-document-probe, native-corpus-triage), total 2949.67s. Both failed with subprocess.TimeoutExpired at 60 seconds in tools/check_corpus.py invocations; document-probe cleanup additionally hit WinError 32 on a child-held stderr.log. No functional assertion failed before these timeouts. native-batch, native-bench-batch-statistics, and all DOCX real-world regressions passed. Preserved full first-run detail in cf112-ctest-first-detail.log. Starting ctest --rerun-failed --output-on-failure -j 1 with unchanged source, binaries, assertions and timeouts.\n')
+log = base/'cf112-retry.log'
+print('Starting serial retry; log:', log, flush=True)
+with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    p = subprocess.run(['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '1', '--rerun-failed', '--output-on-failure'], cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)

+print(data[-18000:], flush=True)
+with (repo/'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')

+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:

+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())

CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()

+serialized = json.dumps(layers)

+for header in headers:

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+branch = 'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+assert git('branch', '--show-current').decode().strip() == branch
+assert not git('ls-files', '-u')
+subprocess.run(['git', 'fetch', 'origin', 'main'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+subprocess.run(['git', 'merge-base', '--is-ancestor', 'origin/main', 'HEAD'], cwd=repo, check=True)
+ctest = (repo.parent / 'cf112-ctest.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+match = re.search(r'(\d+)% tests passed, (\d+) tests failed out of (\d+)', ctest)
+assert match and match[2] == '2' and match[3] == '404', 'Validate the retained full-suite result'
+failed_names = set(re.findall(r'Test\s+#\d+:\s+(\S+).*?\*\*\*Failed', ctest))
+assert failed_names == {'native-document-probe', 'native-corpus-triage'}, failed_names
+for test, filename in [('native-document-probe', 'cf112-retry.log'), ('native-corpus-triage', 'cf112-triage-retry.log')]:
+    retry = (repo.parent / filename).read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    assert '100% tests passed' in retry and '***' not in retry, (test, retry)
+    assert re.search(r'Test\s+#\d+:\s+' + re.escape(test) + r'\s+\.+\s+Passed', retry), test
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    assert data == git('show', f'8d34bbc6:{name}'), name
+    assert data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'), name
+assert hashlib.sha256((repo / 'tests/check_reading_release.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest() == '6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b'
+changed = git('diff', '--name-only').decode().splitlines()
+assert changed == ['OUT-CF.txt'], changed
+assert not git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only')
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt', 'RESOLVE.md']:
+    assert not re.search(rb'^(?:<<<<<<< |=======$|>>>>>>> )', (repo / name).read_bytes(), re.M), name
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write(f'\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 7: pre-push verification]\nFull requested build succeeded. Initial CTest: 402/{match[3]} passed; native-document-probe and native-corpus-triage hit 60-second subprocess timeouts. Each failed test subsequently passed serially by verified test name with unchanged source, binaries, assertions and timeout values. Combined result: {match[3]}/{match[3]} unique tests passed, outstanding failures: none; only sdk-release excluded as requested. Retained cf112-ctest.log, cf112-ctest-first-detail.log, cf112-retry.log and cf112-triage-retry.log. Re-fetched origin/main and verified it is fully contained in HEAD (0 incoming commits). No unmerged paths or conflict markers in resolved reports. Three protected files remain byte-identical to recovery HEAD 8d34bbc6 and origin/main; reading-release SHA256 remains 6eb22a304686b2160d1f283a709936bfdfd65c3b0f5e10bb060f457c4faffb2b. +from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, hashlib, json
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+def git(*args):
+    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=repo)
+for name in ['OUT2.txt', 'RESOLVE-OUT.txt']:
+    p = repo / name
+    lines = p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True)
+    resolved = b''.join(line for line in lines if not line.startswith((b'<<<<<<< ', b'=======', b'>>>>>>> ')))
+    for stage in [2, 3]:
+        side = git('show', f':{stage}:{name}').splitlines()
+        remaining = iter(resolved.splitlines())
+        assert all(any(candidate == line for candidate in remaining) for line in side), (name, stage)
+    p.write_bytes(resolved)
+    print(name, ': retained all lines from both stages')
+with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery step 2: main merge resolved]\nMerged origin/main fe9adf43 into the existing PR head branch. Conflicts: OUT2.txt (add/add) and RESOLVE-OUT.txt (content); removed only conflict marker lines, retained both sides in order, verified each side as a line subsequence. CMakeLists.txt, docs/API.md and tools/layers.json merged automatically. No source/API conflict. Prior merge 2a314a41 conflict records RESOLVE.md and RESOLVE-OUT.txt retained.\n')
+for name in ['tests/check_reading_release.py', 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json', 'tools/check_ci_hygiene.py']:
+    data = (repo / name).read_bytes()
+    print(name, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), 'unchanged from HEAD:', data == git('show', f'HEAD:{name}'), 'matches main:', data == git('show', f'origin/main:{name}'))
+layers = json.loads((repo / 'tools/layers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+serialized = json.dumps(layers)
+headers = [p for p in git('diff', '--cached', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=A', '--', 'src/*.h').decode().splitlines()]
+for header in headers:
+    assert header in serialized, header
+print('New main headers registered:', headers)
+cache = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\exr-build-cf112\CMakeCache.txt')
+for line in cache.read_text().splitlines():
+    if line.startswith(('EXR_ENABLE_AAC:', 'FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_', 'CMAKE_C_COMPILER:', 'CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER:')):

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)

+from pathlib import Path

+            pass

Preserved full first-run detail in cf112-ctest-first-detail.log. Starting ctest --rerun-failed --output-on-failure -j 1 with unchanged source, binaries, assertions and timeouts.\n')
+log = base/'cf112-retry.log'
+print('Starting serial retry; log:', log, flush=True)
+with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+    p = subprocess.run(['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '1', '--rerun-failed', '--output-on-failure'], cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)

+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess, re, json, time
+
+repo = Path(r'C:\Users\Oscar\Documents\Codex\EXReader-S1-20261109')
+build = repo.parent / 'exr-build-cf112'
+def note(text):
+    with (repo / 'OUT-CF.txt').open('a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        f.write('\n[2026-11-11 recovery validation]\n' + text + '\n')
+
+config = (repo.parent / 'cf112-configure.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+assert 'Build files have been written to:' in config, 'Configuration has not succeeded'
+assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'branch', '--show-current'], cwd=repo).strip() == b'codex/sdk-batch-perf-20261109'
+note('CMake configure succeeded with Windows shell ON, AAC ON, pinned mbedtls source and external Windows-trust CA bundle. SDK docs: 472/472 documented; SDK version consistency PASS (3.42.0, ABI 1, 552 existing literal exceptions); CI hygiene --base origin/main PASS, violations=0. Starting cmake --build exr-build-cf112 -j 2.')
+for label, args in [('build', ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', '2']), ('ctest', ['ctest', '--test-dir', str(build), '-j', '2', '-E', '^sdk-release$'])]:
+    log = repo.parent / f'cf112-{label}.log'
+    print(label, 'started; log:', log, flush=True)
+    with log.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        process = subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=repo, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+        while process.poll() is None:
+            time.sleep(5)
+    data = log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    print(label, 'exit:', process.returncode, flush=True)
+    print(data[-14000:], flush=True)
+    note(f'{label}: exit={process.returncode}; log={log}.\n' + data[-1600:])
+    if process.returncode:
+        raise SystemExit(process.returncode)
+print('Build and requested CTest finished successfully.', flush=True)
